===== M0016 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 392-436
Documentation:

Method code:
392 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
393 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
394 |       cursor++;
395 |       --ungetCursor;
396 |       if (charno == -1) {
397 |         charno = getOffset();
398 |       }
399 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
400 |     }
401 | 
402 |     for(;;) {
403 |       int c;
404 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
405 |         hitEOF = true;
406 |         if (charno == -1) {
407 |           charno = getOffset();
408 |         }
409 |         return EOF_CHAR;
410 |       }
411 |       cursor++;
412 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
413 | 
414 | 
415 |       if (c <= 127) {
416 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
417 |           lineEndChar = c;
418 |           c = '\n';
419 |         }
420 |       } else {
421 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
422 |           continue;
423 |         }
424 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
425 |           lineEndChar = c;
426 |           c = '\n';
427 |         }
428 |       }
429 | 
430 |       if (charno == -1) {
431 |         charno = getOffset();
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |       return c;
435 |     }
436 |   }

===== M0024 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 443-459
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
443 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
444 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
445 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
446 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
447 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
448 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
449 |         String inputName = input.getName();
450 | 
451 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
452 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
453 |         // will get an error down the line.
454 |         inputs.add(input);
455 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
456 |       }
457 |     }
458 |     return inputs;
459 |   }

===== M0026 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.normalizeObjectLiteralKeyAnnotations(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 211-218
Documentation:
/**
     * In the AST that Rhino gives us, it needs to make a distinction
     * between jsdoc on the object literal node and jsdoc on the object literal
     * value. For example,
     * <pre>
     * var x = {
     *   / JSDOC /
     *   a: 'b',
     *   c: / JSDOC / 'd'
     * };
     * </pre>
     *
     * But in few narrow cases (in particular, function literals), it's
     * a lot easier for us if the doc is attached to the value.
     */
Method code:
211 |     private void normalizeObjectLiteralKeyAnnotations(
212 |         Node objlit, Node key, Node value) {
213 |       Preconditions.checkState(objlit.isObjectLit());
214 |       if (key.getJSDocInfo() != null &&
215 |           value.isFunction()) {
216 |         value.setJSDocInfo(key.getJSDocInfo());
217 |       }
218 |     }

83 |   public void setPositionInformation(int startLineno, int startCharno,
84 |                                      int endLineno, int endCharno) {
85 |     if (startLineno == endLineno) {

99 |     }
100 |

/**
   * Builds a {@link JSDocInfo} object based on the populated information and
   * returns it. Once this method is called, the builder can be reused to build
   * another {@link JSDocInfo} object.
   *
   * @param associatedNode The source node containing the JSDoc.
   * @return a {@link JSDocInfo} object populated with the values given to this
   *     builder. If no value was populated, this method simply returns
   *     {@code null}
   */
Method code:
124 |   public JSDocInfo build(Node associatedNode) {
125 |     if (populated) {
126 |       JSDocInfo built = currentInfo;
127 |       built.setAssociatedNode(associatedNode);
128 |       populateDefaults(built);
129 |       populated = false;
130 |       currentInfo = new JSDocInfo(this.parseDocumentation);
131 |       return built;
132 |     } else {
133 |       return null;
134 |     }
135 |   }

165 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
166 |                                    StaticSourceFile sourceFile,

===== M0070 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.runCallable(Callable<T>,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 594-646
Documentation:

Method code:
594 |   @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
595 |   static <T> T runCallable(
596 |       final Callable<T> callable, boolean useLargeStackThread, boolean trace) {
597 | 
598 |     // Under JRE 1.6, the jscompiler overflows the stack when running on some
599 |     // large or complex js code. Here we start a new thread with a larger
600 |     // stack in order to let the compiler do its thing, without having to
601 |     // increase the stack size for *every* thread (which is what -Xss does).
602 |     // Might want to add thread pool support for clients that compile a lot.
603 | 
604 |     final boolean dumpTraceReport = trace;
605 |     final Object[] result = new Object[1];
606 |     final Throwable[] exception = new Throwable[1];
607 |     Runnable runnable = new Runnable() {
608 |       @Override
609 |       public void run() {
610 |         try {
611 |           if (dumpTraceReport) {
612 |             Tracer.initCurrentThreadTrace();
613 |           }
614 |           result[0] = callable.call();
615 |         } catch (Throwable e) {
616 |           exception[0] = e;
617 |         } finally {
618 |           if (dumpTraceReport) {
619 |             Tracer.logAndClearCurrentThreadTrace();
620 |           }
621 |         }
622 |       }
623 |     };
624 | 
625 |     if (useLargeStackThread) {
626 |       Thread th = new Thread(null, runnable, "jscompiler", COMPILER_STACK_SIZE);
627 |       th.start();
628 |       while (true) {
629 |         try {
630 |           th.join();
631 |           break;
632 |         } catch (InterruptedException ignore) {
633 |           // ignore
634 |         }
635 |       }
636 |     } else {
637 |       runnable.run();
638 |     }
639 | 
640 |     // Pass on any exception caught by the runnable object.
641 |     if (exception[0] != null) {
642 |       throw new RuntimeException(exception[0]);
643 |     }
644 | 
645 |     return (T) result[0];
646 |   }

===== M0074 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.extractMultilineTextualBlock(JsDocToken,WhitespaceOption)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1320-1416
Documentation:
/**
   * Extracts the text found on the current line and all subsequent
   * until either an annotation, end of comment or end of file is reached.
   * Note that if this method detects an end of line as the first token, it
   * will quit immediately (indicating that there is no text where it was
   * expected).  Note that token = info.token; should be called after this
   * method is used to update the token properly in the parser.
   *
   * @param token The start token.
   * @param option How to handle whitespace.
   *
   * @return The extraction information.
   */
Method code:
1320 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
1321 |   private ExtractionInfo extractMultilineTextualBlock(JsDocToken token,
1322 |                                                       WhitespaceOption option) {
1323 | 
1324 |     if (token == JsDocToken.EOC || token == JsDocToken.EOL ||
1325 |         token == JsDocToken.EOF) {
1326 |       return new ExtractionInfo("", token);
1327 |     }
1328 | 
1329 |     stream.update();
1330 |     int startLineno = stream.getLineno();
1331 |     int startCharno = stream.getCharno() + 1;
1332 | 
1333 |     // Read the content from the first line.
1334 |     String line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1335 |     if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1336 |       line = line.trim();
1337 |     }
1338 | 
1339 |     StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();
1340 |     builder.append(line);
1341 | 
1342 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
1343 |     token = next();
1344 | 
1345 |     boolean ignoreStar = false;
1346 | 
1347 |     do {
1348 |       switch (token) {
1349 |         case STAR:
1350 |           if (!ignoreStar) {
1351 |             if (builder.length() > 0) {
1352 |               builder.append(' ');
1353 |             }
1354 | 
1355 |             builder.append('*');
1356 |           }
1357 | 
1358 |           token = next();
1359 |           continue;
1360 | 
1361 |         case EOL:
1362 |           if (option != WhitespaceOption.SINGLE_LINE) {
1363 |             builder.append("\n");
1364 |           }
1365 | 
1366 |           ignoreStar = true;
1367 |           token = next();
1368 |           continue;
1369 | 
1370 |         case ANNOTATION:
1371 |         case EOC:
1372 |         case EOF:
1373 |           // When we're capturing a license block, annotations
1374 |           // in the block are ok.
1375 |           if (!(option == WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE &&
1376 |                 token == JsDocToken.ANNOTATION)) {
1377 |             String multilineText = builder.toString();
1378 | 
1379 |             if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1380 |               multilineText = multilineText.trim();
1381 |             }
1382 | 
1383 |             int endLineno = stream.getLineno();
1384 |             int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1385 | 
1386 |             if (multilineText.length() > 0) {
1387 |               jsdocBuilder.markText(multilineText, startLineno, startCharno,
1388 |                   endLineno, endCharno);
1389 |             }
1390 | 
1391 |             return new ExtractionInfo(multilineText, token);
1392 |           }
1393 | 
1394 |           // FALL THROUGH
1395 | 
1396 |         default:
1397 |           ignoreStar = false;
1398 |           state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
1399 | 
1400 |           if (builder.length() > 0) {
1401 |             builder.append(' ');
1402 |           }
1403 | 
1404 |           builder.append(toString(token));
1405 | 
1406 |           line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1407 | 
1408 |           if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1409 |             line = trimEnd(line);
1410 |           }
1411 | 
1412 |           builder.append(line);
1413 |           token = next();
1414 |       }
1415 |     } while (true);
1416 |   }

===== M0079 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getRemainingJSDocLine()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 200-224
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the remaining JSDoc line without the {@link JsDocToken#EOL},
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOF} or {@link JsDocToken#EOC}.
   */
Method code:
200 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
201 |   String getRemainingJSDocLine() {
202 |     int c;
203 |     for (;;) {
204 |       c = getChar();
205 |       switch (c) {
206 |         case '*':
207 |           if (peekChar() != '/') {
208 |             addToString(c);
209 |             break;
210 |           }
211 |           // fall through
212 |         case EOF_CHAR:
213 |         case '\n':
214 |           ungetChar(c);
215 |           this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
216 |           stringBufferTop = 0;
217 |           return this.string;
218 | 
219 |         default:
220 |           addToString(c);
221 |           break;
222 |       }
223 |     }
224 |   }

===== M0086 =====

/**
     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
223 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
224 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(n);
225 | 
226 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
227 |       // which are var_args.
228 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
229 |       for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
230 |            arg != null;
231 |            arg = arg.getNext()) {
232 |         String argName = arg.getString();
233 |         JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
234 |             null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
235 | 
236 |         if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
237 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
238 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
239 |         }
240 |         if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
241 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
242 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
243 |         }
244 |       }
245 |     }

/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
181 |   boolean parse() {
182 |     int lineno;
183 |     int charno;
184 | 
185 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
186 |     JSTypeExpression type;
187 | 
188 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
189 |     skipEOLs();
190 | 
191 |     JsDocToken token = next();
192 | 
193 |     List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes = Lists.newArrayList();
194 | 
195 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
196 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
197 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
198 |       token = blockInfo.token;
199 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
200 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
201 |       }
202 |     } else {
203 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
204 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
205 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
206 |         // what it was.
207 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
208 |       }
209 |     }
210 | 
211 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
212 |     retry: for (;;) {
213 |       switch (token) {
214 |         case ANNOTATION:
215 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
216 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
217 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
218 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
219 | 
220 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
221 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
222 |             if (annotation == null) {
223 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
224 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
225 |             } else {
226 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
227 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
228 | 
229 |               switch (annotation) {
230 |                 case AUTHOR:
231 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
232 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
233 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
234 | 
235 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
236 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
237 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
238 |                     } else {
239 |                       jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
240 |                     }
241 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
242 |                   } else {
243 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
244 |                   }
245 |                   continue retry;
246 | 
247 |                 case CONSISTENTIDGENERATOR:
248 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConsistentIdGenerator()) {
249 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.consistidgen",
250 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
251 |                   }
252 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
253 |                   continue retry;
254 | 
255 |                 case CONSTANT:
256 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
257 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
258 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
259 |                   }
260 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
261 |                   continue retry;
262 | 
263 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
264 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
265 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
266 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
267 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
268 |                     } else {
269 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
270 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
271 |                     }
272 |                   }
273 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
274 |                   continue retry;
275 | 
276 |                 case DEPRECATED:
277 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
278 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
279 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
280 |                   }
281 | 
282 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
283 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
284 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
285 | 
286 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
287 | 
288 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
289 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
290 |                   }
291 | 
292 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
293 |                   continue retry;
294 | 
295 |                 case INTERFACE:
296 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
297 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
298 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
299 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
300 |                     } else {
301 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
302 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
303 |                     }
304 |                   }
305 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
306 |                   continue retry;
307 | 
308 |                 case DESC:
309 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.isDescriptionRecorded()) {
310 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
311 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
312 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
313 |                     continue retry;
314 |                   } else {
315 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
316 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
317 | 
318 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
319 | 
320 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDescription(description);
321 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
322 |                     continue retry;

326 |                   String fileOverview = "";
327 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
328 |                     ExtractionInfo fileOverviewInfo =
329 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
330 |                             WhitespaceOption.TRIM);
331 | 
332 |                     fileOverview = fileOverviewInfo.string;

334 |                     token = fileOverviewInfo.token;

336 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);

342 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
343 |                   }
344 |                   continue retry;

347 |                 case PRESERVE:

349 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,

351 | 
352 |                   String preserve = preserveInfo.string;

612 |                   boolean isBracketedParam = JsDocToken.LB == token;
613 |                   if (isBracketedParam) {
614 |                     token = next();
615 |                   }

618 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.missing.variable.name",
619 |                         lineno, charno);

627 |                       // annotation.  It makes no sense under our type
628 |                       // system.
629 |                       if (JsDocToken.EQUALS == token) {
630 |                         token = next();
631 |                         if (JsDocToken.STRING == token) {
632 |                           token = next();
633 |                         }
634 |                       }

636 |                       if (JsDocToken.RB != token) {
637 |                         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rb");
638 |                       } else if (type != null) {
639 |                         // Make the type expression optional, if it isn't
640 |                         // already.
641 |                         type = JSTypeExpression.makeOptionalArg(type);
642 |                       }

646 |                     // quietly. We do not handle the JsDocToolkit method
647 |                     // for handling properties of params.
648 |                     if (name.indexOf('.') > -1) {
649 |                       name = null;

651 |                       if (jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
652 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.dup.variable.name", name,
653 |                             lineno, charno);

656 |                             lineno, charno);

683 |                   }

685 | 
686 |                 case PRESERVE_TRY:

752 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.seemissing",
753 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
754 |                     } else {
755 |                       jsdocBuilder.addReference(reference);
756 |                     }
757 | 
758 |                     token = referenceInfo.token;
759 |                   } else {
760 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
761 |                   }
762 |                   continue retry;
763 | 
764 |                 case SUPPRESS:
765 |                   token = parseSuppressTag(next());
766 |                   continue retry;
767 | 
768 |                 case TEMPLATE:
769 |                   ExtractionInfo templateInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
770 |                   String templateTypeName = templateInfo.string;
771 | 
772 |                   if (templateTypeName.length() == 0) {
773 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.templatemissing",
774 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
775 |                   } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTemplateTypeName(
776 |                       templateTypeName)) {
777 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.template.at.most.once",
778 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
779 |                   }
780 | 
781 |                   token = templateInfo.token;
782 |                   continue retry;
783 | 
784 |                 case IDGENERATOR:
785 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordIdGenerator()) {
786 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.idgen",
787 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
788 |                   }
789 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
790 |                   continue retry;
791 | 
792 |                 case VERSION:
793 |                   ExtractionInfo versionInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
794 |                   String version = versionInfo.string;
795 | 
796 |                   if (version.length() == 0) {
797 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.versionmissing",
798 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
799 |                   } else {
800 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVersion(version)) {
801 |                        parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.extraversion",
802 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
803 |                     }
804 |                   }
805 | 
806 |                   token = versionInfo.token;
807 |                   continue retry;
808 | 
809 |                 case DEFINE:
810 |                 case RETURN:
811 |                 case THIS:
812 |                 case TYPE:
813 |                 case TYPEDEF:
814 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
815 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
816 | 
817 |                   Node typeNode = null;
818 |                   if (!lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() &&
819 |                       annotation == Annotation.RETURN) {
820 |                     // If RETURN doesn't have a type annotation, record
821 |                     // it as the unknown type.
822 |                     typeNode = newNode(Token.QMARK);
823 |                   } else {
824 |                     skipEOLs();
825 |                     token = next();
826 |                     typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token);
827 |                   }
828 | 
829 |                   if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
830 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
831 |                     if (typeNode != null && token != JsDocToken.LC) {
832 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
833 |                     }
834 |                   }
835 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
836 | 
837 |                   if (type == null) {
838 |                     // error reported during recursive descent
839 |                     // recovering parsing
840 |                   } else {
841 |                     switch (annotation) {
842 |                       case DEFINE:
843 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
844 |                           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
845 |                               lineno, charno);
846 |                         }
847 |                         break;
848 | 
849 |                       case RETURN:
850 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
851 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
852 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
853 |                           break;
854 |                         }
855 | 
856 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
857 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
858 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
859 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
860 | 
861 |                           String returnDescription =
862 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
863 | 
864 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
865 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
866 |                                 returnDescription);
867 |                           }
868 | 
869 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
870 |                         } else {
871 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
872 |                         }
873 |                         continue retry;
874 | 
875 |                       case THIS:
876 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
877 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
878 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
879 |                         }
880 |                         break;
881 | 
882 |                       case TYPE:
883 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
884 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
885 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
886 |                         }
887 |                         break;
888 | 
889 |                       case TYPEDEF:
890 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
891 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
892 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
893 |                         }
894 |                         break;
895 |                     }
896 |                   }
897 | 
898 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
899 |                   continue retry;
900 |               }
901 |             }
902 |           }
903 |           break;
904 | 
905 |         case EOC:
906 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
907 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
908 |           }
909 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
910 |           return true;
911 | 
912 |         case EOF:
913 |           // discard any accumulated information
914 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
915 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
916 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
917 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
918 |           return false;
919 | 
920 |         case EOL:
921 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
922 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
923 |           }
924 |           token = next();
925 |           continue retry;
926 | 
927 |         default:
928 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
929 |             token = next();
930 |             continue retry;
931 |           } else {
932 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
933 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
934 |             continue retry;
935 |           }
936 |       }
937 | 
938 |       // next token
939 |       token = next();
940 |     }
941 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 232-295
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
232 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
233 |     this.options = options;
234 |     if (errorManager == null) {
235 |       if (outStream == null) {
236 |         setErrorManager(
237 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
238 |       } else {
239 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
240 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
241 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
242 |         setErrorManager(printer);
243 |       }
244 |     }
245 | 
246 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
247 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
248 |       options.checkTypes = true;
249 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
250 |       options.checkTypes = false;
251 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
252 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
253 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
254 |       // parser type warnings.
255 |       options.setWarningLevel(
256 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
257 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
258 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
259 |     }
260 | 
261 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
262 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
263 |       options.setWarningLevel(
264 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
265 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
266 |     }
267 | 
268 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
269 |       options.setWarningLevel(
270 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
271 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
272 |     }
273 | 
274 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
275 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
276 |     guards.add(
277 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
278 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
279 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
280 | 
281 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
282 | 
283 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
284 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
285 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
286 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
287 |     // error off.
288 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
289 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
290 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
291 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
292 |     }
293 | 
294 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
295 |   }

===== M0101 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 510-545
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
510 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
511 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
513 | 
514 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
515 | 
516 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
517 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
518 | 
519 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
520 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
521 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
522 |     }
523 | 
524 |     curNode = n;
525 |     pushScope(n);
526 | 
527 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
528 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
529 |       // scope.
530 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
534 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
535 | 
536 |     // Args
537 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
538 | 
539 |     // Body
540 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
541 |             body.isBlock());
542 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
543 | 
544 |     popScope();
545 |   }

===== M0114 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 609-673
Documentation:

Method code:
609 |     @Override
610 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
611 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
612 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
613 |       if (name == null) {
614 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
615 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
616 |           errorReporter.error(
617 |             "unnamed function statement",
618 |             sourceName,
619 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
620 | 
621 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
622 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
623 |         }
624 |         name = new Name();
625 |         name.setIdentifier("");
626 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
627 |       }
628 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
629 |       Node newName = transform(name);
630 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
631 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
632 |         // declaration.
633 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
634 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
635 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
636 |         // same line as the function keyword.
637 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
638 |             functionNode.getLp();
639 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
640 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
641 |       }
642 | 
643 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
644 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
645 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
646 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
647 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
648 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
649 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
650 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
651 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
652 |       if (fnName != null) {
653 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
654 |       } else {
655 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
656 |       }
657 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
658 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
659 | 
660 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
661 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
662 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
663 |       }
664 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
665 | 
666 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
667 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
668 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
669 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
670 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
671 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
672 |      return node;

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1610 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1611 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1612 |     if (typeExpr != null) {
1613 |       // top-level unions are allowed
1614 |       if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1615 |         next();
1616 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1617 |           // We support double pipes for backwards-compatibility.
1618 |           next();
1619 |         }
1620 |         skipEOLs();
1621 |         token = next();
1622 |         return parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(token, typeExpr);
1623 |       }
1624 |     }
1625 |     return typeExpr;
1626 |   }

===== M0123 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 336-390
Documentation:

Method code:
336 |   protected int getChar() {
337 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
338 |       cursor++;
339 |       --ungetCursor;
340 |       if (charno == -1) {
341 |         charno = getOffset();
342 |       }
343 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     for(;;) {
347 |       int c;
348 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
349 |         hitEOF = true;
350 |         if (charno == -1) {
351 |           charno = getOffset();
352 |         }
353 |         return EOF_CHAR;
354 |       }
355 |       cursor++;
356 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
357 | 
358 | 
359 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
360 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
361 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
362 |           continue;
363 |         }
364 |         lineEndChar = -1;
365 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
366 |         lineno++;
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       if (c <= 127) {
370 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
371 |           lineEndChar = c;
372 |           c = '\n';
373 |         }
374 |       } else {
375 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
376 |           continue;
377 |         }
378 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
379 |           lineEndChar = c;
380 |           c = '\n';
381 |         }
382 |       }
383 | 
384 |       if (charno == -1) {
385 |         charno = getOffset();
386 |       }
387 | 
388 |       return c;
389 |     }
390 |   }

===== M0132 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1706-1732
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1706 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1707 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1708 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1709 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1710 |       skipEOLs();
1711 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1712 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1713 |       skipEOLs();
1714 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1715 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1716 |       skipEOLs();
1717 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1718 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1719 |       String string = stream.getString();
1720 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1721 |         skipEOLs();
1722 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1723 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1724 |         return newStringNode(string);
1725 |       } else {
1726 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1727 |       }
1728 |     }
1729 | 
1730 |     restoreLookAhead(token);
1731 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1732 |   }

===== M0171 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1177-1185
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1177 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1178 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1179 |       return -1;
1180 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1181 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1182 |     } else {
1183 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1184 |     }
1185 |   }

===== M0187 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SourceFile.getLine(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SourceFile.java
Original range: 205-250
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the source line for the indicated line number.
   *
   * @param lineNumber the line number, 1 being the first line of the file.
   * @return The line indicated. Does not include the newline at the end
   *     of the file. Returns {@code null} if it does not exist,
   *     or if there was an IO exception.
   */
Method code:
205 |   public String getLine(int lineNumber) {
206 |     String js = "";
207 |     try {
208 |       // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, this is optimized for few warnings.
209 |       // This is probably the right trade-off, but will be slow if there
210 |       // are lots of warnings in one file.
211 |       js = getCode();
212 |     } catch (IOException e) {
213 |       return null;
214 |     }
215 | 
216 |     int pos = 0;
217 |     int startLine = 1;
218 | 
219 |     // If we've saved a previous offset and it's for a line less than the
220 |     // one we're searching for, then start at that point.
221 |     if (lineNumber >= lastLine) {
222 |       pos = lastOffset;
223 |       startLine = lastLine;
224 |     }
225 | 
226 |     for (int n = startLine; n < lineNumber; n++) {
227 |       int nextpos = js.indexOf('\n', pos);
228 |       if (nextpos == -1) {
229 |         return null;
230 |       }
231 |       pos = nextpos + 1;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     // Remember this offset for the next search we do.
235 |     lastOffset = pos;
236 |     lastLine = lineNumber;
237 | 
238 |     if (js.indexOf('\n', pos) == -1) {
239 |       // If next new line cannot be found, there are two cases
240 |       // 1. pos already reaches the end of file, then null should be returned
241 |       // 2. otherwise, return the contents between pos and the end of file.
242 |       if (pos >= js.length()) {
243 |         return null;
244 |       } else {
245 |         return js.substring(pos, js.length());
246 |       }
247 |     } else {
248 |       return js.substring(pos, js.indexOf('\n', pos));
249 |     }
250 |   }

===== M0188 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 398-411
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
398 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
399 |       Node ret;
400 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
401 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
402 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
403 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
404 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
405 |       } else {
406 |         ret = transform(n);
407 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
408 |       }
409 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
410 |       return ret;
411 |     }

===== M0194 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1659-1700
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1659 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1660 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1661 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1662 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1663 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1664 |       // {?} - right curly
1665 |       // {?=} - equals
1666 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1667 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1668 |       // {function(number, ...[?])} - right bracket
1669 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1670 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1671 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1672 |       token = next();
1673 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1674 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1675 |           token == JsDocToken.RB ||
1676 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1677 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1678 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1679 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1680 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1681 |       }
1682 | 
1683 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1684 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1685 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1686 |     } else {
1687 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1688 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1689 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1690 |           next();
1691 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1692 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1693 |           next();
1694 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1695 |         }
1696 |       }
1697 | 
1698 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1699 |     }
1700 |   }

===== M0197 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSSpace(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 309-316
Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
309 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
310 |     if (c <= 127) {
311 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
312 |     } else {
313 |       return c == 0xA0
314 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
315 |     }
316 |   }

88 |   private static Map<String, Annotation> buildAnnotationNames(

379 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments) {
380 |         final String defaultResource

386 |         ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
387 | 
388 |         String formatString;
389 |         try {
390 |             formatString = rb.getString(messageId);
391 |         } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {
392 |             throw new RuntimeException
393 |                 ("no message resource found for message property "+ messageId);
394 |         }
395 |

398 |          * we need to format it anyway, to make double ''s collapse to
399 |          * single 's.
400 |          */
401 |         // TODO: MessageFormat is not available on pJava
402 |         MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);

   * Get the JSDocInfo for a function.

Method code:
2812 |   public static JSDocInfo getFunctionJSDocInfo(Node n) {
2813 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
2814 |     JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
2815 |     if (fnInfo == null && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
2816 |       // Look for the info on other nodes.

2818 |       if (parent.isAssign()) {

2823 |         fnInfo = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
2824 |       }

2826 |     return fnInfo;

===== M0239 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1219-1369
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1219 |   Node parseInputs() {
1220 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1221 | 
1222 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1223 |     // individual file parse trees.
1224 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1225 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1226 |     }
1227 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1228 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1229 |     }
1230 | 
1231 |     // Parse main js sources.
1232 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1233 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1234 | 
1235 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1236 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1237 | 
1238 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1239 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1240 | 
1241 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1242 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1243 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1244 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1245 |     }
1246 | 
1247 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1248 | 
1249 |     try {
1250 |       // Parse externs sources.
1251 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1252 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1253 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1254 |           return null;
1255 |         }
1256 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1257 |       }
1258 | 
1259 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1260 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1261 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1262 |       }
1263 | 
1264 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1265 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1266 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1267 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1268 | 
1269 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1270 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1271 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1272 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1273 |           }
1274 |         }
1275 | 
1276 |         try {
1277 |           inputs =
1278 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1279 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1280 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1281 |           report(JSError.make(
1282 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1283 | 
1284 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1285 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1286 |             return null;
1287 |           }
1288 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1289 |           report(JSError.make(
1290 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1291 | 
1292 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1293 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1294 |             return null;
1295 |           }
1296 |         }
1297 |       }
1298 | 
1299 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1300 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1301 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1302 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1303 | 
1304 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1305 |         if (n == null) {
1306 |           continue;
1307 |         }
1308 | 
1309 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1310 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1311 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1312 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1313 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1314 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1315 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1316 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1317 | 
1318 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1319 | 
1320 |             externs.add(input);
1321 |             staleInputs = true;
1322 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1323 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1324 |             staleInputs = true;
1325 |           }
1326 |         }
1327 |       }
1328 | 
1329 |       if (staleInputs) {
1330 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1331 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1332 |       }
1333 | 
1334 |       // Build the AST.
1335 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1336 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1337 |         if (n == null) {
1338 |           continue;
1339 |         }
1340 | 
1341 |         if (devMode) {
1342 |           runSanityCheck();
1343 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1344 |             return null;
1345 |           }
1346 |         }
1347 | 
1348 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1349 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1350 | 
1351 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1352 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1353 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1354 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1355 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1356 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1357 |         }
1358 | 
1359 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1360 |       }
1361 | 
1362 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1363 |         return null;
1364 |       }
1365 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1366 |     } finally {
1367 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1368 |     }
1369 |   }

===== M0240 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.compileInternal()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 648-693
Documentation:

Method code:
648 |   private void compileInternal() {
649 |     setProgress(0.0);
650 |     parse();
651 |     // 15 percent of the work is assumed to be for parsing (based on some
652 |     // minimal analysis on big JS projects, of course this depends on options)
653 |     setProgress(0.15);
654 |     if (hasErrors()) {
655 |       return;
656 |     }
657 | 
658 |     if (!precheck()) {
659 |       return;
660 |     }
661 | 
662 |     if (options.nameAnonymousFunctionsOnly) {
663 |       // TODO(nicksantos): Move this into an instrument() phase maybe?
664 |       check();
665 |       return;
666 |     }
667 | 
668 |     if (!options.skipAllPasses) {
669 |       check();
670 |       if (hasErrors()) {
671 |         return;
672 |       }
673 | 
674 |       if (options.isExternExportsEnabled()
675 |           || options.externExportsPath != null) {
676 |         externExports();
677 |       }
678 | 
679 |       // IDE-mode is defined to stop here, before the heavy rewriting begins.
680 |       if (!options.ideMode) {
681 |         optimize();
682 |       }
683 |     }
684 | 
685 |     if (options.recordFunctionInformation) {
686 |       recordFunctionInformation();
687 |     }
688 | 
689 |     if (options.devMode == DevMode.START_AND_END) {
690 |       runSanityCheck();
691 |     }
692 |     setProgress(1.0);
693 |   }

604 |     }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LightweightMessageFormatter.java
Original range: 69-114

Method code:
69 |   private String format(JSError error, boolean warning) {
70 |     // extract source excerpt
71 |     SourceExcerptProvider source = getSource();
72 |     String sourceExcerpt = source == null ? null :
73 |         excerpt.get(
74 |             source, error.sourceName, error.lineNumber, excerptFormatter);
75 | 
76 |     // formatting the message
77 |     StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder();
78 |     if (error.sourceName != null) {
79 |       b.append(error.sourceName);
80 |       if (error.lineNumber > 0) {
81 |         b.append(':');
82 |         b.append(error.lineNumber);
83 |       }
84 |       b.append(": ");
85 |     }
86 | 
87 |     b.append(getLevelName(warning ? CheckLevel.WARNING : CheckLevel.ERROR));
88 |     b.append(" - ");
89 | 
90 |     b.append(error.description);
91 |     b.append('\n');
92 |     if (sourceExcerpt != null) {
93 |       b.append(sourceExcerpt);
94 |       b.append('\n');
95 |       int charno = error.getCharno();
96 | 
97 |       // padding equal to the excerpt and arrow at the end
98 |       // charno == sourceExpert.length() means something is missing
99 |       // at the end of the line
100 |       if (excerpt.equals(LINE)
101 |           && 0 <= charno && charno <= sourceExcerpt.length()) {
102 |         for (int i = 0; i < charno; i++) {
103 |           char c = sourceExcerpt.charAt(i);
104 |           if (Character.isWhitespace(c)) {
105 |             b.append(c);
106 |           } else {
107 |             b.append(' ');
108 |           }
109 |         }
110 |         b.append("^\n");
111 |       }
112 |     }
113 |     return b.toString();
114 |   }

/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place
   * holder {0} with a wild card that matches all possible strings.

===== M0262 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1739-1774
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1739 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1740 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1741 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1742 |     }
1743 | 
1744 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1745 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1746 |     int charno = stream.getCharno();
1747 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1748 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1749 |       skipEOLs();
1750 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1751 |         next();
1752 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1753 |       }
1754 |     }
1755 | 
1756 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName, lineno, charno);
1757 | 
1758 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1759 |       next();
1760 |       skipEOLs();
1761 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1762 |       if (memberType != null) {
1763 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1764 | 
1765 |         skipEOLs();
1766 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1767 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1768 |         }
1769 | 
1770 |         next();
1771 |       }
1772 |     }
1773 |     return typeNameNode;
1774 |   }

===== M0272 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 51-93
Documentation:

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       for (Node current = node;
56 |            current != null;
57 |            current = current.getParent()) {
58 |         int type = current.getType();
59 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
60 | 
61 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
62 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
63 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
64 |         // a bad idea.
65 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
66 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(current);
67 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
68 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
69 |         } else if (type == Token.ASSIGN) {
70 |           Node rhs = current.getLastChild();
71 |           if (rhs.isFunction()) {
72 |             info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(rhs);
73 |           }
74 |         }
75 | 
76 |         if (info != null) {
77 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
78 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
79 | 
80 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
81 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
82 |             if (guard != null) {
83 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
84 |               if (newLevel != null) {
85 |                 return newLevel;
86 |               }
87 |             }
88 |           }
89 |         }
90 |       }
91 |     }
92 |     return null;
93 |   }

===== M0274 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 459-474
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
459 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
460 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
461 |       Set<String> directives = null;
462 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
463 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
464 |         if (directives == null) {
465 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
466 |         } else {
467 |           directives.add(directive);
468 |         }
469 |       }
470 | 
471 |       if (directives != null) {
472 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
473 |       }
474 |     }

/**
   * Creates a JsDocInfoParser and parses the JsDoc string.
   *
   * Used both for handling individual JSDoc comments and for handling
   * file-level JSDoc comments (@fileoverview and @license).
   *
   * @param node The JsDoc Comment node to parse.

/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0293 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 58-194
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
58 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
59 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
60 |     int c;
61 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
62 |     for (;;) {
63 |       // eat white spaces
64 |       for (;;) {
65 |         charno = -1;
66 |         c = getChar();
67 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
68 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
69 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
71 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
72 |           break;
73 |         }
74 |       }
75 | 
76 |       switch (c) {
77 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
78 |         case '@':
79 |           do {
80 |             c = getChar();
81 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
82 |               addToString(c);
83 |             } else {
84 |               ungetChar(c);
85 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
86 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
87 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
88 |             }
89 |           } while (true);
90 | 
91 |         case '*':
92 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
93 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
94 |           } else {
95 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
96 |           }
97 | 
98 |         case ',':
99 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
100 | 
101 |         case '>':
102 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
103 | 
104 |         case '(':
105 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
106 | 
107 |         case ')':
108 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
109 | 
110 |         case '{':
111 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
112 | 
113 |         case '}':
114 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
115 | 
116 |         case '[':
117 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
118 | 
119 |         case ']':
120 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
121 | 
122 |         case '?':
123 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
124 | 
125 |         case '!':
126 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
127 | 
128 |         case ':':
129 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
130 | 
131 |         case '=':
132 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
133 | 
134 |         case '|':
135 |           matchChar('|');
136 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
137 | 
138 |         case '.':
139 |           c = getChar();
140 |           if (c == '<') {
141 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
142 |           } else {
143 |             if (c == '.') {
144 |               c = getChar();
145 |               if (c == '.') {
146 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
147 |               } else {
148 |                 addToString('.');
149 |               }
150 |             }
151 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
152 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
153 |             c = '.';
154 |           }
155 |           // fall through
156 | 
157 |         default: {
158 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
159 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
160 |           int c1 = c;
161 |           addToString(c);
162 |           int c2 = getChar();
163 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
164 |             ungetChar(c2);
165 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
166 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
167 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
168 |           } else {
169 |             do {
170 |               c1 = c2;
171 |               c2 = getChar();
172 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
173 |                 ungetChar(c2);
174 |                 ungetChar(c1);
175 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
176 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
177 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
178 |               } else {
179 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
180 |                   addToString(c1);
181 |                 } else {
182 |                   ungetChar(c2);
183 |                   addToString(c1);
184 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
185 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
186 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
187 |                 }
188 |               }
189 |             } while (true);
190 |           }
191 |         }
192 |       }
193 |     }
194 |   }

Method code:
241 |   private void addToString(int c) {
242 |     int N = stringBufferTop;
243 |     if (N == stringBuffer.length) {
244 |         char[] tmp = new char[stringBuffer.length * 2];
245 |         System.arraycopy(stringBuffer, 0, tmp, 0, N);
246 |         stringBuffer = tmp;
247 |     }
248 |     stringBuffer[N] = (char)c;
249 |     stringBufferTop = N + 1;
250 |   }

===== M0313 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 769-806
Documentation:

Method code:
769 |     @Override
770 |     Node processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral literalNode) {
771 |       if (literalNode.isDestructuring()) {
772 |         reportDestructuringAssign(literalNode);
773 |       }
774 | 
775 |       Node node = newNode(Token.OBJECTLIT);
776 |       for (ObjectProperty el : literalNode.getElements()) {
777 |         if (config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
778 |           if (el.isGetter()) {
779 |             reportGetter(el);
780 |             continue;
781 |           } else if (el.isSetter()) {
782 |             reportSetter(el);
783 |             continue;
784 |           }
785 |         }
786 | 
787 |         Node key = transformAsString(el.getLeft());
788 |         Node value = transform(el.getRight());
789 |         if (el.isGetter()) {
790 |           key.setType(Token.GETTER_DEF);
791 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.isFunction());
792 |           if (getFnParamNode(value).hasChildren()) {
793 |             reportGetterParam(el.getLeft());
794 |           }
795 |         } else if (el.isSetter()) {
796 |           key.setType(Token.SETTER_DEF);
797 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.isFunction());
798 |           if (!getFnParamNode(value).hasOneChild()) {
799 |             reportSetterParam(el.getLeft());
800 |           }
801 |         }
802 |         key.addChildToFront(value);
803 |         node.addChildToBack(key);
804 |       }
805 |       return node;
806 |     }

===== M0315 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 476-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
476 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
477 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
478 |     int type = n.getType();
479 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
480 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
481 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
486 | 
487 |     switch (type) {
488 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
489 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       default:
493 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
494 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
495 |           // would no longer point to the true next
496 |           Node next = child.getNext();
497 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
498 |           child = next;
499 |         }
500 |         break;
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     curNode = n;
504 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
505 |   }

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 |     compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(acceptEs5);
126 | 
127 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
128 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
129 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
130 | 
131 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
132 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
133 |     try {
134 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
135 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
136 |       logger.info(
137 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
138 |     } finally {
139 |       Context.exit();
140 |     }
141 |     Node root = null;
142 |     if (astRoot != null) {
143 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
144 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
145 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
146 |     }
147 |     return root;
148 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 781-794
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
781 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
782 |     if (item == null) {
783 |       return null;
784 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
785 |       return item.getNext();
786 |     } else {
787 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
788 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
789 |         return item.chain(result);
790 |       } else {
791 |         return item;
792 |       }
793 |     }
794 |   }
