===== M0339 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseModifiesTag(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1012-1051
Documentation:
/**
   * Parse a {@code @modifies} tag of the form
   * {@code @modifies&#123;this|arguments|param&#125;}.
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   */
Method code:
1012 |   private JsDocToken parseModifiesTag(JsDocToken token) {
1013 |     if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1014 |       Set<String> modifies = new HashSet<String>();
1015 |       while (true) {
1016 |         if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1017 |           String name = stream.getString();
1018 |           if (!modifiesAnnotationKeywords.contains(name)
1019 |               && !jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
1020 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies.unknown", name,
1021 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
1022 |           }
1023 | 
1024 |           modifies.add(stream.getString());
1025 |           token = next();
1026 |         } else {
1027 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies",
1028 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
1029 |           return token;
1030 |         }
1031 | 
1032 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1033 |           token = next();
1034 |         } else {
1035 |           break;
1036 |         }
1037 |       }
1038 | 
1039 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1040 |         parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies",
1041 |             stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
1042 |       } else {
1043 |         token = next();
1044 |         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordModifies(modifies)) {
1045 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies.duplicate",
1046 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
1047 |         }
1048 |       }
1049 |     }
1050 |     return token;
1051 |   }

===== M0349 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 128-179
Documentation:

Method code:
128 |     @Override
129 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
130 |       // null is the smallest value
131 |       if (p2 == null) {
132 |         if (p1 == null) {
133 |           return 0;
134 |         } else {
135 |           return P1_GT_P2;
136 |         }
137 |       }
138 | 
139 |       // check level
140 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
141 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
142 |       }
143 | 
144 |       // sourceName comparison
145 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
146 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
147 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
148 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
149 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
150 |           return sourceCompare;
151 |         }
152 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
153 |         return P1_LT_P2;
154 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
155 |         return P1_GT_P2;
156 |       }
157 |       // lineno comparison
158 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
159 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
160 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
161 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
162 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
163 |         return P1_LT_P2;
164 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
165 |         return P1_GT_P2;
166 |       }
167 |       // charno comparison
168 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
169 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
170 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
171 |         return charno1 - charno2;
172 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
173 |         return P1_LT_P2;
174 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
175 |         return P1_GT_P2;
176 |       }
177 |       // description
178 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
179 |     }

248 |     // very useful to find out at least which function caused the exception.

===== M0374 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1902-1904
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1902 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1903 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1904 |   }

===== M0375 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsAst.parse(AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsAst.java
Original range: 83-105
Documentation:

Method code:
83 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {
84 |     try {
85 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceFile.getName());
86 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
87 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
88 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
89 |           logger_);
90 |     } catch (IOException e) {
91 |       compiler.report(
92 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
93 |     }
94 | 
95 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
96 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
97 |       root = IR.script();
98 |     } else {
99 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
103 |     // the source file and module.
104 |     root.setStaticSourceFile(sourceFile);
105 |   }

/**
   * Looks for a parameter type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @param lineno The line of the type expression.
   * @param startCharno The starting character position of the type expression.
   * @param matchingLC Whether the type expression starts with a "{".
   * @param onlyParseSimpleNames If true, only simple type names are parsed
   *     (via a call to parseTypeNameAnnotation instead of
   *     parseTypeExpressionAnnotation).
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
1134 |   private Node parseAndRecordTypeNode(JsDocToken token, int lineno,
1135 |                                       int startCharno,
1136 |                                       boolean matchingLC,
1137 |                                       boolean onlyParseSimpleNames) {
1138 |     Node typeNode = null;
1139 | 
1140 |     if (onlyParseSimpleNames) {
1141 |       typeNode = parseTypeNameAnnotation(token);
1142 |     } else {
1143 |       typeNode = parseTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
1144 |     }
1145 | 
1146 |     if (typeNode != null && !matchingLC) {
1147 |       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
1148 |     }
1149 | 
1150 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1151 |       int endLineno = stream.getLineno();
1152 |       int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1153 | 
1154 |       jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(
1155 |           typeNode, lineno, startCharno, endLineno, endCharno, matchingLC);
1156 |     }
1157 | 
1158 |     return typeNode;
1159 |   }

===== M0390 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.lookAheadForTypeAnnotation()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2320-2336
Documentation:
/**
   * Look ahead for a type annotation by advancing the character stream.
   * Does not modify the token stream.
   * This is kind of a hack, and is only necessary because we use the token
   * stream to parse types, but need the underlying character stream to get
   * JsDoc descriptions.
   * @return Whether we found a type annotation.
   */
Method code:
2320 |   private boolean lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() {
2321 |     boolean matchedLc = false;
2322 |     int c;
2323 |     while (true) {
2324 |       c = stream.getChar();
2325 |       if (c == ' ') {
2326 |         continue;
2327 |       } else if (c == '{') {
2328 |         matchedLc = true;
2329 |         break;
2330 |       } else {
2331 |         break;
2332 |       }
2333 |     }
2334 |     stream.ungetChar(c);
2335 |     return matchedLc;
2336 |   }

===== M0413 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.extractBlockComment(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1430-1475
Documentation:
/**
   * Extracts the top-level block comment from the JsDoc comment, if any.
   * This method differs from the extractMultilineTextualBlock in that it
   * terminates under different conditions (it doesn't have the same
   * prechecks), it does not first read in the remaining of the current
   * line and its conditions for ignoring the "*" (STAR) are different.
   *
   * @param token The starting token.
   *
   * @return The extraction information.
   */
Method code:
1430 |   private ExtractionInfo extractBlockComment(JsDocToken token) {
1431 |     StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();
1432 | 
1433 |     boolean ignoreStar = true;
1434 | 
1435 |     do {
1436 |       switch (token) {
1437 |         case ANNOTATION:
1438 |         case EOC:
1439 |         case EOF:
1440 |           return new ExtractionInfo(builder.toString().trim(), token);
1441 | 
1442 |         case STAR:
1443 |           if (!ignoreStar) {
1444 |             if (builder.length() > 0) {
1445 |               builder.append(' ');
1446 |             }
1447 | 
1448 |             builder.append('*');
1449 |           }
1450 | 
1451 |           token = next();
1452 |           continue;
1453 | 
1454 |         case EOL:
1455 |           ignoreStar = true;
1456 |           builder.append('\n');
1457 |           token = next();
1458 |           continue;
1459 | 
1460 |         default:
1461 |           if (!ignoreStar && builder.length() > 0) {
1462 |             builder.append(' ');
1463 |           }
1464 | 
1465 |           ignoreStar = false;
1466 | 
1467 |           builder.append(toString(token));
1468 | 
1469 |           String line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1470 |           line = trimEnd(line);
1471 |           builder.append(line);
1472 |           token = next();
1473 |       }
1474 |     } while (true);
1475 |   }
